M11 · Oh My Pi · a53e4e790d39

指令执行：从 /goal 到回执

一个内置指令如何被解析、路由、执行并写回会话？

第 11 / 11 章8 条源码 findingmain
本章路线问题→比喻→多条源码证据→四问→源码目录→交互图→逐行翻译
先用一个生活比喻

指令像机场值机单：先认出航班，再检查登机规则，交给正确柜台执行，最后把登机和到达状态写回系统。

先回答本章问题

一个内置指令如何被解析、路由、执行并写回会话？ 内层发动机负责每一步，外层管家负责一步结束后判断要不要重试、压缩、换模型、继续目标或等待后台工作。

本章证据板：8 条 finding

不要把一段代码当成整个系统；下面每张卡分别说明事实、白话、工程影响、边界和可点击的固定源码。

01
L1factarchitecture

01 · 核心是强化 Agent loop，产品层再叠加大型 Session maintenance 状态机

源码事实

packages/agent 处理模型、工具、steering 和 telemetry；产品 AgentSession 在 agent_end 后按优先级处理 yield、空 stop、goal compaction、异常 stop、stream stall、model fallback、todo、async wake 和 session_stop。

白话解释

内层发动机负责每一步，外层管家负责一步结束后判断要不要重试、压缩、换模型、继续目标或等待后台工作。

对自研 Harness 的含义

自治恢复能力强，但 session maintenance 已成为复杂调度器，修改顺序容易产生竞态。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 879–897
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 898–918
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  938  function emitInputMessages(stream: EventStream<AgentEvent, AgentMessage[]>, messages: readonly AgentMessage[]): void {
  939  	for (const message of messages) {
  940  		stream.push({ type: "message_start", message });
  941  		stream.push({ type: "message_end", message });
  942  	}
  943  }
  944
  945  /**
  946   * Resolve aside entries at the moment the loop is about to inject them. Each entry
  947   * is either a ready {@link AgentMessage} or a sync thunk evaluated here so the
  948   * producer can make the final inject-or-drop decision (return null) against
  949   * up-to-the-injection state — e.g. dropping late diagnostics a newer edit
  950   * superseded. Kept sync so it can never stall the loop.
  951   */
  952  function resolveAsides(entries: AsideMessage[] | undefined): AgentMessage[] {
  953  	if (!entries || entries.length === 0) return [];
  954  	const out: AgentMessage[] = [];
  955  	for (const entry of entries) {
  956  		const message = typeof entry === "function" ? entry() : entry;
  957  		if (message) out.push(message);
  958  	}
  959  	return out;
  960  }
  961
  962  async function runLoopBody(
  963  	currentContext: AgentContext,
  964  	newMessages: AgentMessage[],
  965  	config: AgentLoopConfig,
  966  	signal: AbortSignal | undefined,
  967  	stream: EventStream<AgentEvent, AgentMessage[]>,
  968  	telemetry: AgentTelemetry | undefined,
  969  	invokeAgentSpan: Span | undefined,
  970  	stepCounter: StepCounter,
  971  	initialMessages: AgentMessage[],
  972  	streamFn?: StreamFn,
  973  ): Promise<void> {
  974  	let deadlineTimer: Timer | undefined;
  975  	if (config.deadline !== undefined) {
  976  		const deadlineAbortController = new AbortController();
  977  		const deadlineReason = new DOMException("Deadline exceeded", "TimeoutError");
  978  		const delay = config.deadline - Date.now();
  979  		if (delay <= 0) {
  980  			deadlineAbortController.abort(deadlineReason);
  981  		} else {
  982  			deadlineTimer = setTimeout(() => {
  983  				deadlineAbortController.abort(deadlineReason);
  984  			}, delay);
  985  		}
  986  		signal = signal ? AbortSignal.any([signal, deadlineAbortController.signal]) : deadlineAbortController.signal;
  987  	}
  988
  989  	const softRequirementState = config.softToolRequirementState ?? { escalations: 0 };

  884  function extractIntent(args: Record<string, unknown>): { intent?: string; strippedArgs: Record<string, unknown> } {
  885  	const { [INTENT_FIELD]: intent, ...strippedArgs } = args;
  886  	if (typeof intent !== "string") {
  887  		return { strippedArgs };
  888  	}
  889  	const trimmed = intent.trim();
  890  	return { intent: trimmed.length > 0 ? trimmed : undefined, strippedArgs };
  891  }

  897  	currentContext: AgentContext,
  898  	newMessages: AgentMessage[],
  899  	config: AgentLoopConfig,
  900  	signal: AbortSignal | undefined,
  901  	stream: EventStream<AgentEvent, AgentMessage[]>,

  905  	const telemetry = resolveTelemetry(config.telemetry, config.sessionId);
  906  	const invokeAgentSpan = startInvokeAgentSpan(telemetry, config.model);
  907  	const stepCounter = { count: 0 };

  909  	try {
  910  		await runInActiveSpan(invokeAgentSpan, () =>
  911  			runLoopBody(
  912  				currentContext,
  913  				newMessages,
  914  				config,
  915  				signal,
  916  				stream,

  920  				initialMessages,

  922  			),
  923  		);

  925  		caughtError = err;
  926  		throw err;
  927  	} finally {
  928  		finishInvokeAgentSpan(telemetry, invokeAgentSpan, {
  929  			stepCount: stepCounter.count,
  930  			errorObject: caughtError,
  931  		});
  932  	}
  933  }

  952  function emitInputMessages(stream: EventStream<AgentEvent, AgentMessage[]>, messages: readonly AgentMessage[]): void {
  953  	for (const message of messages) {
  954  		stream.push({ type: "message_start", message });
  955  		stream.push({ type: "message_end", message });
  956  	}
  957  }

  959  /**
  960   * Resolve aside entries at the moment the loop is about to inject them. Each entry
  961   * is either a ready {@link AgentMessage} or a sync thunk evaluated here so the
  962   * producer can make the final inject-or-drop decision (return null) against
  963   * up-to-the-injection state — e.g. dropping late diagnostics a newer edit
  964   * superseded. Kept sync so it can never stall the loop.
  965   */
  966  function resolveAsides(entries: AsideMessage[] | undefined): AgentMessage[] {
  967  	if (!entries || entries.length === 0) return [];
  968  	const out: AgentMessage[] = [];
  969  	try {
  970  		for (const entry of entries) {
  971  			const message = typeof entry === "function" ? entry() : entry;
  972  			if (message) out.push(message);
  973  		}
  974  	} catch (error) {
  975  		discardAsides(out, error instanceof Error ? error : new Error(String(error)));
  976  		throw error;
  977  	}
  978  	return out;
  979  }
  980
  981  function discardAsides(messages: readonly AgentMessage[], error: Error): void {
  982  	for (const message of messages) {
  983  		(message as CommittableAsideMessage)[ASIDE_MESSAGE_DISCARD]?.(error);
  984  	}
  985  }

02
L1factarchitecture

02 · steering 不只在轮间排队，还能在工具执行中协作中断

源码事实

loop 每轮前和 stop boundary 轮询 steering/asides；工具批运行时同时使用 hard abort 与 cooperative steeringSignal，interruptible 等待工具会被 steer 打断，普通有副作用工具只收到软信号并在边界注入消息。

白话解释

用户插话时，纯等待可以立刻停；正在改文件的工具不会粗暴半路杀死，而是完成到安全边界再让模型听新指令。

对自研 Harness 的含义

中断语义按工具类型区分，明显优于统一 AbortController。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 999–1022
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 1023–1048
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

03
L1factarchitecture

03 · 工具调度支持 shared/exclusive 并发和完整 pre-dispatch 改写

源码事实

tool calls 在流结束前完成参数校验与 beforeToolCall，改写后的参数回写 assistant snapshot；执行时 shared calls 并发，exclusive 会等待之前 exclusive 和所有 shared；afterToolCall 可规范化结果。

白话解释

先把所有施工单审核、改好并固化，再按“可并行/独占”排程，日志看到的参数就是实际执行参数。

对自研 Harness 的含义

既能并发提速，也能避免 edit/bash 等副作用互撞；第三方结果还在单一边界做防腐。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 2067–2132
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 2133–2200
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

 2068  interface PreparedToolCall {
 2069  	tool: AgentTool<any> | undefined;
 2070  	/** Validated (possibly hook-revised) execution args; raw args when validation failed. */
 2071  	args: Record<string, unknown>;
 2072  	validationErrorMessage?: string;
 2073  	blocked?: boolean;
 2074  	blockReason?: string;
 2075  	prepareError?: unknown;
 2076  }

 2078  /**
 2079   * Prepare results computed in the stream-done branch (before `message_start`/
 2080   * `message_end`) so a `beforeToolCall` args revision is baked into the message
 2081   * every consumer snapshots. `executeToolCalls` consumes them; a message that
 2082   * bypassed the streamed path (e.g. Harmony-recovered) is prepared at dispatch
 2083   * time instead.
 2084   */
 2085  const preparedDispatchByMessage = new WeakMap<AssistantMessage, Map<string, PreparedToolCall>>();

 2087  function resolveToolForCall(
 2088  	tools: AgentTool<any>[] | undefined,
 2089  	toolCall: AgentToolCall,
 2090  	resolveFallbackTool: AgentLoopConfig["resolveFallbackTool"],
 2091  ): AgentTool<any> | undefined {
 2092  	// Tools emitted via OpenAI's custom-tool path (e.g. `apply_patch` on GPT-5)
 2093  	// come back under their wire-level name, which may differ from the
 2094  	// harness-internal `name`. Match on either, preferring `name` for
 2095  	// determinism if both somehow collide.
 2096  	return (
 2097  		tools?.find(t => t.name === toolCall.name) ??
 2098  		tools?.find(t => t.customWireName !== undefined && t.customWireName === toolCall.name) ??
 2099  		// Not in the advertised set: let the host route side-transport tools
 2100  		// (e.g. xd:// device mounts) called by their top-level name.
 2101  		resolveFallbackTool?.(toolCall.name)
 2102  	);
 2103  }
 2104
 2105  /**
 2106   * Pre-dispatch phase for every pending tool call on `assistantMessage`, run in
 2107   * call order: intent extraction, argument validation, and the `beforeToolCall`
 2108   * hook. A hook `args` revision is revalidated against the tool schema and
 2109   * written back to `toolCall.arguments`; run before `message_start`/`message_end`
 2110   * (the streamed path) that makes the revision the single source of truth —
 2111   * history, execution events, persistence, provider replay, concurrency
 2112   * scheduling, and `tool.execute` all agree. Failures are recorded per call and
 2113   * surfaced by `executeToolCalls` at the record's scheduled slot.
 2114   */
 2115  async function prepareToolCallDispatch(
 2116  	assistantMessage: AssistantMessage,
 2117  	context: AgentContext,
 2118  	config: AgentLoopConfig,
 2119  	signal: AbortSignal | undefined,
 2120  ): Promise<Map<string, PreparedToolCall>> {
 2121  	const { resolveFallbackTool, intentTracing, beforeToolCall } = config;
 2122  	const prepared = new Map<string, PreparedToolCall>();
 2123  	for (const toolCall of assistantMessage.content) {
 2124  		if (toolCall.type !== "toolCall") continue;
 2125  		if ((toolCall as CursorExecResolvedCarrier)[kCursorExecResolved] === true) continue;
 2126  		const tool = resolveToolForCall(context.tools, toolCall, resolveFallbackTool);
 2127  		const entry: PreparedToolCall = { tool, args: toolCall.arguments as Record<string, unknown> };
 2128  		prepared.set(toolCall.id, entry);
 2129  		let argsForExecution = toolCall.arguments as Record<string, unknown>;
 2130  		if (intentTracing) {
 2131  			const { intent, strippedArgs } = extractIntent(toolCall.arguments);
 2132  			argsForExecution = strippedArgs;
 2133  			if (intent) {
 2134  				toolCall.intent = intent;
 2135  			} else if (typeof tool?.intent === "function") {
 2136  				try {
 2137  					const derived = tool.intent(strippedArgs as never)?.trim();
 2138  					if (derived) {
 2139  						toolCall.intent = derived;
 2140  					}
 2141  				} catch {
 2142  					// intent function must never break tool execution
 2143  				}
 2144  			}
 2145  		}
 2146  		const validate = (args: Record<string, unknown>): Record<string, unknown> | undefined => {
 2147  			try {
 2148  				if (!tool) throw new Error(`Tool ${toolCall.name} not found`);
 2149  				return validateToolArguments(tool, { ...toolCall, arguments: args });
 2150  			} catch (validationError) {
 2151  				if (tool?.lenientArgValidation) {
 2152  					const fallback = { ...args };
 2153  					delete fallback.__parseError;
 2154  					delete fallback.__rawJson;
 2155  					return fallback;
 2156  				}
 2157  				entry.args = "__parseError" in args ? { __parseError: args.__parseError } : args;
 2158  				entry.validationErrorMessage =
 2159  					validationError instanceof Error ? validationError.message : String(validationError);
 2160  				return undefined;
 2161  			}
 2162  		};
 2163  		const effectiveArgs = validate(argsForExecution);
 2164  		if (effectiveArgs === undefined) continue;
 2165  		entry.args = effectiveArgs;
 2166  		if (!beforeToolCall || !tool) continue;
 2167  		let beforeResult: BeforeToolCallResult | undefined;
 2168  		try {
 2169  			beforeResult = await beforeToolCall(
 2170  				{ assistantMessage, toolCall, tool, args: effectiveArgs, context },
 2171  				signal,
 2172  			);
 2173  		} catch (e) {
 2174  			// Contract: a throwing hook surfaces as a tool-error result without
 2175  			// aborting the batch — rethrown inside the execution span in runTool.
 2176  			entry.prepareError = e;
 2177  			continue;
 2178  		}
 2179  		if (beforeResult?.block) {
 2180  			entry.blocked = true;
 2181  			entry.blockReason = beforeResult.reason;
 2182  			continue;
 2183  		}
 2184  		if (beforeResult?.args !== undefined) {
 2185  			// Revalidate: a hook revision is untrusted input to the tool schema.
 2186  			const revised = validate(beforeResult.args);
 2187  			if (revised === undefined) continue;
 2188  			// Bake the revision into the message itself. On the streamed path this
 2189  			// precedes every consumer snapshot, so there is exactly one version of
 2190  			// the call anywhere downstream.
 2191  			toolCall.arguments = beforeResult.args;
 2192  			entry.args = revised;
 2193  		}
 2194  	}
 2195  	return prepared;
 2196  }

 2198   * Execute tool calls from an assistant message.
 2199   */

 2247  		: AbortSignal.any([steeringAbortController.signal, ircAbortController.signal]);

 2260  			args: toolCall.arguments as Record<string, unknown>,
 2261  		};

 2266  			try {

 2268  				// argument-dependent policy governs the call that actually runs.

 2107
 2108  /**
 2109   * Prepare results computed in the stream-done branch (before `message_start`/
 2110   * `message_end`) so a `beforeToolCall` args revision is baked into the message
 2111   * every consumer snapshots. `executeToolCalls` consumes them; a message that
 2112   * bypassed the streamed path (e.g. Harmony-recovered) is prepared at dispatch
 2113   * time instead.
 2114   */
 2115  const preparedDispatchByMessage = new WeakMap<AssistantMessage, Map<string, PreparedToolCall>>();
 2116
 2117  function resolveToolForCall(
 2118  	tools: AgentTool<any>[] | undefined,
 2119  	toolCall: AgentToolCall,
 2120  	resolveFallbackTool: AgentLoopConfig["resolveFallbackTool"],
 2121  ): AgentTool<any> | undefined {
 2122  	// Tools emitted via OpenAI's custom-tool path (e.g. `apply_patch` on GPT-5)
 2123  	// come back under their wire-level name, which may differ from the
 2124  	// harness-internal `name`. Match on either, preferring `name` for
 2125  	// determinism if both somehow collide.
 2126  	return (
 2127  		tools?.find(t => t.name === toolCall.name) ??
 2128  		tools?.find(t => t.customWireName !== undefined && t.customWireName === toolCall.name) ??
 2129  		// Not in the advertised set: let the host route side-transport tools
 2130  		// (e.g. xd:// device mounts) called by their top-level name.
 2131  		resolveFallbackTool?.(toolCall.name)
 2132  	);
 2133  }
 2134
 2135  /**
 2136   * Pre-dispatch phase for every pending tool call on `assistantMessage`, run in
 2137   * call order: intent extraction, argument validation, and the `beforeToolCall`
 2138   * hook. A hook `args` revision is revalidated against the tool schema and
 2139   * written back to `toolCall.arguments`; run before `message_start`/`message_end`
 2140   * (the streamed path) that makes the revision the single source of truth —
 2141   * history, execution events, persistence, provider replay, concurrency
 2142   * scheduling, and `tool.execute` all agree. Failures are recorded per call and
 2143   * surfaced by `executeToolCalls` at the record's scheduled slot.
 2144   */
 2145  async function prepareToolCallDispatch(
 2146  	assistantMessage: AssistantMessage,
 2147  	context: AgentContext,
 2148  	config: AgentLoopConfig,
 2149  	signal: AbortSignal | undefined,
 2150  ): Promise<Map<string, PreparedToolCall>> {
 2151  	const { resolveFallbackTool, intentTracing, beforeToolCall } = config;
 2152  	const prepared = new Map<string, PreparedToolCall>();
 2153  	for (const toolCall of assistantMessage.content) {
 2154  		if (toolCall.type !== "toolCall") continue;
 2155  		if ((toolCall as CursorExecResolvedCarrier)[kCursorExecResolved] === true) continue;
 2156  		const tool = resolveToolForCall(context.tools, toolCall, resolveFallbackTool);
 2157  		const entry: PreparedToolCall = { tool, args: toolCall.arguments as Record<string, unknown> };
 2158  		prepared.set(toolCall.id, entry);
 2159  		let argsForExecution = toolCall.arguments as Record<string, unknown>;
 2160  		if (intentTracing) {
 2161  			const { intent, strippedArgs } = extractIntent(toolCall.arguments);
 2162  			argsForExecution = strippedArgs;
 2163  			if (intent) {
 2164  				toolCall.intent = intent;
 2165  			} else if (typeof tool?.intent === "function") {
 2166  				try {
 2167  					const derived = tool.intent(strippedArgs as never)?.trim();
 2168  					if (derived) {
 2169  						toolCall.intent = derived;
 2170  					}
 2171  				} catch {
 2172  					// intent function must never break tool execution
 2173  				}
 2174  			}
 2175  		}
 2176  		const validate = (args: Record<string, unknown>): Record<string, unknown> | undefined => {
 2177  			try {
 2178  				if (!tool) throw new Error(`Tool ${toolCall.name} not found`);
 2179  				return validateToolArguments(tool, { ...toolCall, arguments: args });
 2180  			} catch (validationError) {
 2181  				if (tool?.lenientArgValidation) {
 2182  					const fallback = { ...args };
 2183  					delete fallback.__parseError;
 2184  					delete fallback.__rawJson;
 2185  					return fallback;
 2186  				}
 2187  				entry.args = "__parseError" in args ? { __parseError: args.__parseError } : args;
 2188  				entry.validationErrorMessage =
 2189  					validationError instanceof Error ? validationError.message : String(validationError);
 2190  				return undefined;
 2191  			}
 2192  		};
 2193  		const effectiveArgs = validate(argsForExecution);
 2194  		if (effectiveArgs === undefined) continue;
 2195  		entry.args = effectiveArgs;
 2196  		if (!beforeToolCall || !tool) continue;
 2197  		let beforeResult: BeforeToolCallResult | undefined;
 2198  		try {
 2199  			beforeResult = await beforeToolCall(
 2200  				{ assistantMessage, toolCall, tool, args: effectiveArgs, context },
 2201  				signal,
 2202  			);
 2203  		} catch (e) {
 2204  			// Contract: a throwing hook surfaces as a tool-error result without
 2205  			// aborting the batch — rethrown inside the execution span in runTool.
 2206  			entry.prepareError = e;
 2207  			continue;
 2208  		}
 2209  		if (beforeResult?.block) {
 2210  			entry.blocked = true;
 2211  			entry.blockReason = beforeResult.reason;
 2212  			continue;
 2213  		}
 2214  		if (beforeResult?.args !== undefined) {
 2215  			// Revalidate: a hook revision is untrusted input to the tool schema.
 2216  			const revised = validate(beforeResult.args);
 2217  			if (revised === undefined) continue;
 2218  			// Bake the revision into the message itself. On the streamed path this
 2219  			// precedes every consumer snapshot, so there is exactly one version of
 2220  			// the call anywhere downstream.
 2221  			toolCall.arguments = beforeResult.args;
 2222  			entry.args = revised;
 2223  		}
 2224  	}
 2225  	return prepared;
 2226  }
 2227  /**
 2228   * Execute tool calls from an assistant message.
 2229   */
 2230  async function executeToolCalls(
 2231  	currentContext: AgentContext,
 2232  	assistantMessage: AssistantMessage,
 2233  	signal: AbortSignal | undefined,
 2234  	stream: EventStream<AgentEvent, AgentMessage[]>,
 2235  	config: AgentLoopConfig,
 2236  	telemetry: AgentTelemetry | undefined,
 2237  	invokeAgentSpan: Span | undefined,
 2238  ): Promise<{ toolResults: ToolResultMessage[] }> {
 2239  	const tools = currentContext.tools;
 2240  	const {
 2241  		hasSteeringMessages,
 2242  		hasIrcInterrupts,
 2243  		interruptMode = "immediate",
 2244  		getToolContext,
 2245  		transformToolCallArguments,
 2246  		resolveFallbackTool,
 2247  		afterToolCall,
 2248  	} = config;
 2249  	type ToolCallContent = Extract<AssistantMessage["content"][number], { type: "toolCall" }>;
 2250  	// Defensive: the outer loop already filters exec-resolved blocks before
 2251  	// deciding to invoke `executeToolCalls`, but skip them here too so the
 2252  	// guarantee lives with the code that would re-run the tool.
 2253  	const toolCalls = assistantMessage.content.filter(
 2254  		(c): c is ToolCallContent =>
 2255  			c.type === "toolCall" && (c as CursorExecResolvedCarrier)[kCursorExecResolved] !== true,
 2256  	);
 2257  	const emittedToolResults: ToolResultMessage[] = [];
 2258  	const toolCallInfos = toolCalls.map(call => ({ id: call.id, name: call.name }));
 2259  	const batchId = `${assistantMessage.timestamp ?? Date.now()}_${toolCalls[0]?.id ?? "batch"}`;
 2260  	const shouldInterruptImmediately = interruptMode !== "wait";
 2261  	const steeringAbortController = new AbortController();
 2262  	const ircAbortController = new AbortController();
 2263  	// Cooperative channel: aborted when queued steering (or an interrupting
 2264  	// peer IRC) is detected mid-batch. Tools receive it via tool context
 2265  	// (`ctx.steeringSignal`) and MAY react — e.g. an auto-backgroundable bash
 2266  	// backgrounds itself so the message injects promptly — but it never kills
 2267  	// anything; ignoring it is always safe.
 2268  	const steeringSoftController = new AbortController();
 2269  	// Interruptible tools (pure waits: hub wait, vibe) observe steering +
 2270  	// external + IRC aborts. Every other tool sees ONLY the external signal:
 2271  	// neither queued steering nor a peer IRC ever hard-kills a partially
04
L1facttools

04 · 内建工具面远超文件与 shell

源码事实

registry 包含 read/bash/edit/write、AST grep/edit、LSP、eval kernels、glob/grep、browser、computer、GitHub、web search、image inspect/gen、checkpoint/rewind、todo/goal、task/hub/yield、memory tools、skills 管理等；SDK 再合并 extension/custom/MCP/RPC host tools。

白话解释

它更像一套本地 Agent 操作系统，而不是四件套 coding CLI。

对自研 Harness 的含义

覆盖广但默认 prompt/tool schema 成本和安全面都更大，需要动态激活与分组。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 38–70
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 71–105
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  170  	/** Optional fetch implementation injected into the URL read pipeline (tests, proxies). Defaults to global fetch. */
  171  	fetch?: FetchImpl;
  172  	/** Provider credential resolver forwarded unchanged to restricted child sessions. */
  173  	getApiKey?: AgentOptions["getApiKey"];
  174  	/** Skip subprocess-kernel availability checks and warmup */
  175  	skipPythonPreflight?: boolean;
  176  	/** Pre-loaded context files (AGENTS.md, etc) */
05
L1risktools

05 · Approval 分级完整，但默认是 yolo

源码事实

工具声明 read/write/exec tier 和可按参数计算的 policy；user per-tool allow/prompt/deny 高优先级。always-ask 只自动 read，write 自动 read+write，yolo 自动所有 tier；settings 默认 tools.approvalMode=yolo。

白话解释

门卫机制很成熟，但默认把门敞开；用户不改设置时，bash、browser、task 等执行级工具通常不询问。

对自研 Harness 的含义

面向个人效率是合理选择，企业默认策略应反转为 write 或 always-ask。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 13–25
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 26–39
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   13  export type ApprovalPolicy = "allow" | "deny" | "prompt";
   14  export type ApprovalMode = "always-ask" | "write" | "yolo";
   15
   16  type ApprovalSubject = Pick<AgentTool, "name" | "approval" | "formatApprovalDetails">;
   17
   18  export interface ResolvedApproval {
   19  	policy: ApprovalPolicy;
   20  	tier: ToolTier;
   21  	reason?: string;
   22  	override: boolean;
   23  	source?: "tool" | "user" | "mode";
   24  }
   25
   26  const POLICY_VALUES: ReadonlySet<ApprovalPolicy> = new Set(["allow", "deny", "prompt"]);
   27  const TIER_VALUES: ReadonlySet<ToolTier> = new Set(["read", "write", "exec"]);
   28
   29  const TIER_RANK: Record<ToolTier, number> = {
   30  	read: 0,
   31  	write: 1,
   32  	exec: 2,
   33  };
   34
   35  const APPROVAL_MODE_MAX_TIER: Record<ApprovalMode, ToolTier> = {
   36  	"always-ask": "read",
   37  	write: "write",
   38  	yolo: "exec",
   39  };
   40
   41  const DEFAULT_PROMPT_TRUNCATE_CHARS = 2000;
   42
   43  /** Best-effort conversion of an arbitrary user-supplied value to a policy. */
   44  function normalizePolicy(value: unknown): ApprovalPolicy | undefined {
   45  	if (typeof value !== "string") return undefined;
   46  	const lowered = value.trim().toLowerCase();
   47  	return POLICY_VALUES.has(lowered as ApprovalPolicy) ? (lowered as ApprovalPolicy) : undefined;
   48  }
   49
   50  function isToolTier(value: unknown): value is ToolTier {
   51  	return typeof value === "string" && TIER_VALUES.has(value as ToolTier);
   52  }
   53
   54  function normalizeDecision(value: unknown): Omit<ResolvedApproval, "policy"> & { policy?: ApprovalPolicy } {
   55  	if (isToolTier(value)) {
   56  		return { tier: value, override: false };
   57  	}
   58
   59  	if (value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)) {
   60  		const record = value as Record<string, unknown>;
   61  		const tier = isToolTier(record.tier) ? record.tier : "exec";
   62  		const reason = typeof record.reason === "string" && record.reason.length > 0 ? record.reason : undefined;
   63  		const policy = normalizePolicy(record.policy);
   64  		return {
   65  			tier,
   66  			override: record.override === true,
   67  			...(policy ? { policy } : {}),
   68  			...(reason ? { reason } : {}),
   69  		};
   70  	}
   71
   72  	return { tier: "exec", override: false };
   73  }
   74
   75  function getToolDecision(
   76  	tool: ApprovalSubject,
   77  	args: unknown,
   78  ): Omit<ResolvedApproval, "policy"> & { policy?: ApprovalPolicy } {
   79  	const approval = tool.approval;
   80  	const decision: ToolApprovalDecision | undefined = typeof approval === "function" ? approval(args) : approval;
   81  	return normalizeDecision(decision);
   82  }
   83
   84  /**
   85   * Evaluate a tool's own approval declaration against `args` and return the
   86   * resulting capability tier, defaulting to `exec` when the tool omits an
   87   * approval. Unlike reading `tool.approval` directly, this runs function-valued
   88   * approvals — the write tool's `xd://` gate uses it to take a mounted device's
   89   * argument-dependent tier instead of falling back to `exec`.
   90   */
   91  export function resolveToolTier(tool: ApprovalSubject, args: unknown): ToolTier {
   92  	return getToolDecision(tool, args).tier;
   93  }
   94
   95  function modeApprovesTier(mode: ApprovalMode, tier: ToolTier): boolean {
   96  	return TIER_RANK[tier] <= TIER_RANK[APPROVAL_MODE_MAX_TIER[mode]];
   97  }
   98
   99  /**
  100   * Resolve approval policy for a tool call.
  101   *
  102   * Resolution order:
  103   *  1. Tool `approval(args)` decision, defaulting to tier "exec" when omitted.
  104   *  2. User per-tool override, if set and valid.
  105   *  3. Active mode tier comparison.
  106   *
  107   * In yolo mode, override-based tool prompts are ignored; user `tools.approval`
  108   * settings remain authoritative.
  109   */
  110  export function resolveApproval(

   27
   28  const POLICY_VALUES: ReadonlySet<ApprovalPolicy> = new Set(["allow", "deny", "prompt"]);
   29  const TIER_VALUES: ReadonlySet<ToolTier> = new Set(["read", "write", "exec"]);

   32  	read: 0,

   46  function normalizePolicy(value: unknown): ApprovalPolicy | undefined {
   47  	if (typeof value !== "string") return undefined;
   48  	const lowered = value.trim().toLowerCase();
   49  	return POLICY_VALUES.has(lowered as ApprovalPolicy) ? (lowered as ApprovalPolicy) : undefined;
   50  }

   53  	return typeof value === "string" && TIER_VALUES.has(value as ToolTier);
   54  }
   55
   56  function normalizeDecision(value: unknown): Omit<ResolvedApproval, "policy"> & { policy?: ApprovalPolicy } {
   57  	if (isToolTier(value)) {
   58  		return { tier: value, override: false };
   59  	}
   60
   61  	if (value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)) {
   62  		const record = value as Record<string, unknown>;
   63  		const tier = isToolTier(record.tier) ? record.tier : "exec";

   65  		const policy = normalizePolicy(record.policy);

   74  		};
   75  	}

   78  }

06
L1limitationtools

06 · 主 Agent 默认宿主执行；子任务 isolation 默认 none 且主要隔离工作区

源码事实

bash runner 使用宿主 shell/native process；源码未给主 Agent 建容器/namespace sandbox。task isolation 可选 APFS/btrfs/ZFS/reflink/overlayfs/ProjFS/block clone/worktree/copy，并用 patch 或 branch 合并，但默认 mode=none。

白话解释

子工人可以拿一份独立项目副本，防止同时改乱代码；这不自动隔离网络、进程和主机秘密，而且默认连副本也不开。

对自研 Harness 的含义

工作区隔离与安全沙箱必须分开标注；无人值守仍需外层 VM/container。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–109
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 110–220
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · ed4c78bc0faa · 打开账本提交 ↗
packages/coding-agent/src/session/bash-runner.ts:1–220固定提交 ↗
    1  import * as path from "node:path";
    2  import type { Agent } from "@oh-my-pi/pi-agent-core";
    3  import { logger } from "@oh-my-pi/pi-utils";
    4  import type { Settings } from "../config/settings";
    5  import { type BashResult, executeBash as executeBashCommand } from "../exec/bash-executor";
    6  import type { ExtensionRunner } from "../extensibility/extensions";
    7  import { outputMeta } from "../tools/output-meta";
    8  import { clampTimeout } from "../tools/tool-timeouts";
    9  import type { BashExecutionMessage } from "./messages";
   10  import type { SessionManager } from "./session-manager";
   11
   12  /** Destination that owns a bash result after a session or branch transition. */
   13  export type BashAppendDestination =
   14  	| { kind: "current"; manager: SessionManager }
   15  	| { kind: "detached"; manager: SessionManager }
   16  	| { kind: "branch"; manager: SessionManager; parentId: string | null };
   17
   18  /** Reference-counted session target captured when a bash execution starts. */
   19  export interface BashSessionTarget {
   20  	sessionId: string;
   21  	refs: number;
   22  	destination?: BashAppendDestination;
   23  	pending?: Promise<BashAppendDestination>;
   24  }
   25
   26  interface PendingBashMessage {
   27  	target: BashSessionTarget;
   28  	message: BashExecutionMessage;
   29  }
   30
   31  /** Ownership snapshot spanning a session or branch transition. */
   32  export interface BashSessionTransition {
   33  	oldTarget: BashSessionTarget;
   34  	newTarget: BashSessionTarget;
   35  	oldSessionId: string;
   36  	oldSessionFile: string | undefined;
   37  	oldLeafId: string | null;
   38  	detachedManager: SessionManager | undefined;
   39  	resolveOld: ((destination: BashAppendDestination) => void) | undefined;
   40  	resolveNew: (destination: BashAppendDestination) => void;
   41  }
   42
   43  /** Capabilities the bash runner borrows from its owning session. */
   44  export interface BashRunnerHost {
   45  	agent: Agent;
   46  	sessionManager: SessionManager;
   47  	settings: Settings;
   48  	extensionRunner(): ExtensionRunner | undefined;
   49  	isStreaming(): boolean;
   50  }
   51
   52  /** Owns bash execution and preserves result ownership across transcript transitions. */
   53  export class BashRunner {
   54  	readonly #host: BashRunnerHost;
   55  	#abortControllers = new Set<AbortController>();
   56  	#pendingMessages: PendingBashMessage[] = [];
   57  	#sessionTarget: BashSessionTarget;
   58
   59  	constructor(host: BashRunnerHost) {
   60  		this.#host = host;
   61  		this.#sessionTarget = {
   62  			sessionId: host.sessionManager.getSessionId(),
   63  			refs: 0,
   64  			destination: { kind: "current", manager: host.sessionManager },
   65  		};
   66  	}
   67
   68  	/** Executes a bash command while retaining the session and branch that owned its start. */
   69  	async executeBash(
   70  		command: string,
   71  		onChunk?: (chunk: string) => void,
   72  		options?: { excludeFromContext?: boolean; useUserShell?: boolean },
   73  	): Promise<BashResult> {
   74  		const target = this.#captureSessionTarget();
   75  		let targetTransferred = false;
   76  		const excludeFromContext = options?.excludeFromContext === true;
   77  		const cwd = this.#host.sessionManager.getCwd();
   78  		try {
   79  			const extensionRunner = this.#host.extensionRunner();
   80  			if (extensionRunner?.hasHandlers("user_bash")) {
   81  				const hookResult = await extensionRunner.emitUserBash({
   82  					type: "user_bash",
   83  					command,
   84  					excludeFromContext,
   85  					cwd,
   86  				});
   87  				if (hookResult?.result) {
   88  					targetTransferred = true;
   89  					await this.#recordResultForTarget(target, command, hookResult.result, options);
   90  					return hookResult.result;
   91  				}
   92  			}
   93
   94  			const abortController = new AbortController();
   95  			this.#abortControllers.add(abortController);
   96  			let result: BashResult;
   97  			try {
   98  				result = await executeBashCommand(command, {
   99  					onChunk,
  100  					signal: abortController.signal,
  101  					sessionKey: target.sessionId,
  102  					cwd,
  103  					timeout: clampTimeout("bash", undefined, this.#host.settings.get("tools.maxTimeout")) * 1000,
  104  					onMinimizedSave: originalText => this.#saveOriginalArtifact(target, originalText),
  105  					useUserShell: options?.useUserShell,
  106  				});
  107  			} finally {
  108  				this.#abortControllers.delete(abortController);
  109  			}
  110  			targetTransferred = true;
  111  			await this.#recordResultForTarget(target, command, result, options);
  112  			return result;
  113  		} finally {
  114  			if (!targetTransferred) await this.#releaseSessionTarget(target);
  115  		}
  116  	}
  117
  118  	/** Records a bash result supplied outside executeBash in the current ownership scope. */
  119  	recordBashResult(command: string, result: BashResult, options?: { excludeFromContext?: boolean }): void {
  120  		const target = this.#captureSessionTarget();
  121  		const message = this.#createMessage(command, result, options);
  122  		if (this.#host.isStreaming() && target === this.#sessionTarget) {
  123  			this.#pendingMessages.push({ target, message });
  124  			return;
  125  		}
  126  		if (target.destination) {
  127  			try {
  128  				this.#appendMessage(target.destination, message);
  129  			} finally {
  130  				void this.#releaseSessionTarget(target);
  131  			}
  132  			return;
  133  		}
  134  		void this.#appendOwnedMessage(target, message).catch(error => {
  135  			logger.error("Failed to record bash result in its owning session", { error: String(error) });
  136  		});
  137  	}
  138
  139  	/** Cancels every running bash command. */
  140  	abort(): void {
  141  		for (const abortController of this.#abortControllers) abortController.abort();
  142  	}
  143
  144  	/** Whether a bash command is currently running. */
  145  	get isRunning(): boolean {
  146  		return this.#abortControllers.size > 0;
  147  	}
  148
  149  	/** Whether bash results are waiting for a safe persistence boundary. */
  150  	get hasPendingMessages(): boolean {
  151  		return this.#pendingMessages.length > 0;
  152  	}
  153
  154  	/** Flushes deferred bash results without changing their captured ownership. */
  155  	async flushPending(): Promise<void> {
  156  		if (this.#pendingMessages.length === 0) return;
  157  		const pending = this.#pendingMessages;
  158  		this.#pendingMessages = [];
  159  		for (const { target, message } of pending) await this.#appendOwnedMessage(target, message);
  160  	}
  161
  162  	/** Runs a leaf rewrite while retaining in-flight bash on its originating branch. */
  163  	withBranchTransition<T>(mutate: () => T): T {
  164  		const transition = this.beginSessionTransition();
  165  		let transitioned = false;
  166  		try {
  167  			const result = mutate();
  168  			this.markSessionTransition(transition);
  169  			transitioned = true;
  170  			return result;
  171  		} finally {
  172  			this.finishSessionTransition(transition, transitioned);
  173  		}
  174  	}
  175
  176  	/** Snapshots the owner of in-flight bash before a session or branch transition. */
  177  	beginSessionTransition(options?: { persistDetached?: boolean }): BashSessionTransition {
  178  		const oldTarget = this.#sessionTarget;
  179  		let detachedManager: SessionManager | undefined;
  180  		let resolveOld: ((destination: BashAppendDestination) => void) | undefined;
  181  		if (oldTarget.refs > 0) {
  182  			detachedManager = this.#host.sessionManager.cloneCurrentSession({ persist: options?.persistDetached });
  183  			const pendingOld = Promise.withResolvers<BashAppendDestination>();
  184  			oldTarget.destination = undefined;
  185  			oldTarget.pending = pendingOld.promise;
  186  			resolveOld = pendingOld.resolve;
  187  		}
  188  		const pendingNew = Promise.withResolvers<BashAppendDestination>();
  189  		return {
  190  			oldTarget,
  191  			newTarget: {
  192  				sessionId: this.#host.sessionManager.getSessionId(),
  193  				refs: 0,
  194  				pending: pendingNew.promise,
  195  			},
  196  			oldSessionId: this.#host.sessionManager.getSessionId(),
  197  			oldSessionFile: this.#host.sessionManager.getSessionFile(),
  198  			oldLeafId: this.#host.sessionManager.getLeafId(),
  199  			detachedManager,
  200  			resolveOld,
  201  			resolveNew: pendingNew.resolve,
  202  		};
  203  	}
  204
  205  	/** Adopts a transition's new target as the live bash owner. */
  206  	markSessionTransition(transition: BashSessionTransition): void {
  207  		transition.newTarget.sessionId = this.#host.sessionManager.getSessionId();
  208  		this.#sessionTarget = transition.newTarget;
  209  	}
  210
  211  	/** Resolves destinations opened by beginSessionTransition. */
  212  	finishSessionTransition(transition: BashSessionTransition, success: boolean): void {
  213  		const manager = this.#host.sessionManager;
  214  		const currentDestination: BashAppendDestination = { kind: "current", manager };
  215  		let oldDestination: BashAppendDestination = currentDestination;
  216  		if (success && transition.resolveOld) {
  217  			const currentFile = manager.getSessionFile();
  218  			const sameFile =
  219  				transition.oldSessionFile === currentFile ||
  220  				(transition.oldSessionFile !== undefined &&
  221  					currentFile !== undefined &&
  222  					path.resolve(transition.oldSessionFile) === path.resolve(currentFile));
  223  			const sameSession = transition.oldSessionId === manager.getSessionId() && sameFile;
  224  			if (sameSession) {
  225  				oldDestination =
  226  					transition.oldLeafId === manager.getLeafId()
  227  						? currentDestination
  228  						: { kind: "branch", manager, parentId: transition.oldLeafId };
  229  			} else if (transition.detachedManager) {
  230  				oldDestination = { kind: "detached", manager: transition.detachedManager };
  231  			}
  232  		}
  233  		if (transition.resolveOld) {
  234  			transition.oldTarget.pending = undefined;
  235  			transition.oldTarget.destination = oldDestination;
  236  			transition.resolveOld(oldDestination);
  237  		}
  238  		transition.newTarget.pending = undefined;
  239  		transition.newTarget.destination = currentDestination;
  240  		if (!success) transition.newTarget.sessionId = manager.getSessionId();
  241  		transition.resolveNew(currentDestination);
  242  		if (transition.detachedManager && (oldDestination.kind !== "detached" || transition.oldTarget.refs === 0)) {
  243  			void transition.detachedManager.close().catch(error => {
  244  				logger.warn("Failed to close detached bash session writer", { error: String(error) });
  245  			});
  246  		}
  247  	}
  248
  249  	async #saveOriginalArtifact(target: BashSessionTarget, originalText: string): Promise<string | undefined> {
  250  		try {
  251  			const destination = target.destination ?? (await target.pending);
  252  			return await destination?.manager.saveArtifact(originalText, "bash-original");
  253  		} catch {
  254  			return undefined;
  255  		}
  256  	}
  257
  258  	#createMessage(
  259  		command: string,
  260  		result: BashResult,
  261  		options?: { excludeFromContext?: boolean },
  262  	): BashExecutionMessage {
  263  		const meta = outputMeta().truncationFromSummary(result, { direction: "tail" }).get();
  264  		return {
  265  			role: "bashExecution",
  266  			command,
  267  			output: result.output,
  268  			exitCode: result.exitCode,
  269  			cancelled: result.cancelled,
  270  			truncated: result.truncated,
  271  			meta,
  272  			timestamp: Date.now(),
  273  			excludeFromContext: options?.excludeFromContext,
  274  		};
  275  	}
  276
  277  	#captureSessionTarget(): BashSessionTarget {
  278  		this.#sessionTarget.refs++;
  279  		return this.#sessionTarget;
  280  	}
  281
  282  	async #releaseSessionTarget(target: BashSessionTarget): Promise<void> {
  283  		if (target.refs <= 0) throw new Error("Bash session target released more than once");
  284  		target.refs--;
  285  		if (target.refs === 0 && target.destination?.kind === "detached") await target.destination.manager.close();
  286  	}

  288  	#appendMessage(destination: BashAppendDestination, message: BashExecutionMessage): void {
  289  		switch (destination.kind) {
  290  			case "current":
  291  				this.#host.agent.appendMessage(message);

07
L1factconnectors

07 · MCP 是完整内建连接器，不是插件样例

源码事实

MCPManager 从多来源配置发现服务器，并行连接、缓存 tools、订阅 resources、接收 notifications、动态刷新；transport 支持 stdio、legacy SSE、Streamable HTTP，HTTP 保存 session id、处理 server request/notification、401/403 刷新后重试。

白话解释

它不仅能调用 MCP 工具，还管理连接、认证、资源订阅、服务端推送和重连生命周期。

对自研 Harness 的含义

连接器成熟度高，适合企业工具生态；同时需审计远端 URL、headers 和本地 command 配置。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 282–330
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 331–380
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  329  	async discoverAndConnect(options?: MCPDiscoverOptions): Promise<MCPLoadResult> {
  330  		let loadedConfigs: LoadMCPConfigsResult;
  331  		try {
  332  			loadedConfigs = await loadAllMCPConfigs(this.cwd, {
  333  				enableProjectConfig: options?.enableProjectConfig,
  334  				filterExa: options?.filterExa,
  335  				filterBrowser: options?.filterBrowser,
  336  			});
  337  		} catch (error) {
  338  			const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
  339  			options?.onStatus?.({ type: "failed", serverName: ".mcp.json", error: message });
  340  			throw error;
  341  		}
  342  		const { configs, exaApiKeys, sources } = loadedConfigs;
  343  		const result = await this.connectServers(configs, sources, options?.onStatus);
  344  		result.exaApiKeys = exaApiKeys;
  345  		return result;
  346  	}
  347
  348  	/**
  349  	 * Connect to specific MCP servers.
  350  	 * Connections are made in parallel for faster startup.
  351  	 */
  352  	async connectServers(
  353  		configs: Record<string, MCPServerConfig>,
  354  		sources: Record<string, SourceMeta>,
  355  		onStatus?: (event: McpConnectionStatusEvent) => void,
  356  	): Promise<MCPLoadResult> {
  357  		type ConnectionTask = {
  358  			name: string;
  359  			config: MCPServerConfig;
  360  			tracked: TrackedPromise<ToolLoadResult>;
  361  			toolsPromise: Promise<ToolLoadResult>;
  362  		};
  363
  364  		const errors = new Map<string, string>();
  365  		const connectedServers = new Set<string>();
  366  		const allTools: CustomTool<TSchema, MCPToolDetails>[] = [];
  367  		const reportedErrors = new Set<string>();
  368  		let allowBackgroundLogging = false;
  369  		const statusServerNames: string[] = [];
  370  		const validationFailures: Array<{ name: string; message: string }> = [];
  371
  372  		// Prepare connection tasks
  373  		const connectionTasks: ConnectionTask[] = [];
  374
  375  		for (const [name, config] of Object.entries(configs)) {
  376  			if (sources[name]) {
  377  				this.#sources.set(name, sources[name]);
  378  				const existing = this.#connections.get(name);
  379  				if (existing) {
  380  					existing._source = sources[name];
  381  				}
  382  			}
  383
  384  			// Skip if already connected
  385  			if (this.#connections.has(name)) {
  386  				connectedServers.add(name);
  387  				continue;
  388  			}
  389
  390  			if (
  391  				this.#pendingConnections.has(name) ||
  392  				this.#pendingToolLoads.has(name) ||
  393  				this.#pendingReconnections.has(name)
  394  			) {
  395  				continue;
  396  			}
  397
  398  			statusServerNames.push(name);
  399
  400  			// Validate config
  401  			const validationErrors = validateServerConfig(name, config);
  402  			if (validationErrors.length > 0) {
  403  				const message = validationErrors.join("; ");
  404  				errors.set(name, message);
  405  				validationFailures.push({ name, message });
  406  				reportedErrors.add(name);
  407  				continue;
  408  			}
  409
  410  			// Save config early so reconnection works even if the initial connect times out
  411  			// and falls back to cached/deferred tools.
  412  			this.#serverConfigs.set(name, config);
  413
  414  			// Resolve auth config before connecting, but do so per-server in parallel.
  415  			const connectionPromise = (async () => {
  416  				const resolvedConfig = await this.#resolveAuthConfig(config);
  417  				return connectToServer(name, resolvedConfig, {
  418  					onNotification: (method, params) => {
  419  						this.#handleServerNotification(name, method, params);
  420  					},
  421  					onRequest: (method, params) => {
  422  						return this.#handleServerRequest(method, params);
  423  					},
  424  				});
  425  			})().then(
  426  				connection => {
  427  					// Store original config (without resolved tokens) to keep
  428  					// cache keys stable and avoid leaking rotating credentials.
  429  					connection.config = config;
  430  					this.#serverConfigs.set(name, config);
  431  					if (sources[name]) {
  432  						connection._source = sources[name];
  433  					}
  434  					if (this.#pendingConnections.get(name) === connectionPromise) {
  435  						this.#pendingConnections.delete(name);
  436  						this.#connections.set(name, connection);
  437  					}
  438
  439  					// Wire auth refresh for HTTP-like transports so 401s trigger token refresh.
  440  					// Gate on a resolvable managed credential, not on the auth block:
  441  					// definition-only configs (url-keyed fallback) get Bearer injection
  442  					// too and need the same mid-session refresh hook.
  443  					if (
  444  						isAuthRefreshableMCPTransport(connection.transport) &&
  445  						lookupMcpOAuthCredential(this.#authStorage, config)
  446  					) {
  447  						connection.transport.onAuthError = async () => {
  448  							const refreshed = await this.#resolveAuthConfig(config, { forceRefresh: true });
  449  							if (refreshed.type === "http" || refreshed.type === "sse") {
  450  								return refreshed.headers ?? null;
  451  							}

  291  	/**
  292  	 * Set a callback to fire when any server's tools change.
  293  	 *
  294  	 * May return a Promise; if so, {@link refreshServerTools} awaits it so that
  295  	 * downstream consumers (e.g. `mcp_notification` listeners for
  296  	 * `notifications/tools/list_changed`) observe not just the manager's
  297  	 * refreshed tool set but also any session-level rebind driven by the
  298  	 * handler (`session.refreshMCPTools`). Other callsites (initial connect,

  300  	 * chains don't need to serialize on the rebind.

  326  	#subscribeAndTrack(name: string, connection: MCPServerConnection, uris: string[], notificationEpoch: number): void {
  327  		void subscribeToResources(connection, uris)
  328  			.then(() => {
  329  				const action = resolveSubscriptionPostAction(
  330  					this.#notificationsEnabled,
  331  					this.#notificationsEpoch,
  332  					notificationEpoch,
  333  				);
  334  				if (action === "rollback") {
  335  					void unsubscribeFromResources(connection, uris).catch(error => {
  336  						logger.debug("Failed to rollback stale MCP resource subscription", {
  337  							path: `mcp:${name}`,
  338  							error,
  339  						});
  340  					});
  341  					return;
  342  				}
  343  				if (action === "ignore") {
  344  					return;
  345  				}
  346  				this.#subscribedResources.set(name, new Set(uris));
  347  			})
  348  			.catch(error => {
  349  				logger.debug("Failed to subscribe to MCP resources", { path: `mcp:${name}`, error });
  350  			});
  351  	}
  352
  353  	setNotificationsEnabled(enabled: boolean): void {
  354  		const wasEnabled = this.#notificationsEnabled;
  355  		this.#notificationsEnabled = enabled;
  356  		if (enabled === wasEnabled) return;
  357
  358  		this.#notificationsEpoch += 1;
  359  		const notificationEpoch = this.#notificationsEpoch;
  360
  361  		if (enabled) {
  362  			// Subscribe to all connected servers that support it
  363  			for (const [name, connection] of this.#connections) {
  364  				if (connection.capabilities.resources?.subscribe && connection.resources) {
  365  					const uris = connection.resources.map(r => r.uri);
  366  					this.#subscribeAndTrack(name, connection, uris, notificationEpoch);
  367  				}
  368  			}
  369  			return;
  370  		}
  371
  372  		// Unsubscribe from all servers
  373  		for (const [name, connection] of this.#connections) {
  374  			const uris = this.#subscribedResources.get(name);
  375  			if (uris && uris.size > 0) {
  376  				void unsubscribeFromResources(connection, Array.from(uris)).catch(error => {
  377  					logger.debug("Failed to unsubscribe MCP resources", { path: `mcp:${name}`, error });
  378  				});
  379  			}
  380  		}
  381  		this.#subscribedResources.clear();
  382  	}

  401  		let loadedConfigs: LoadMCPConfigsResult;
  402  		try {
  403  			loadedConfigs = await loadAllMCPConfigs(this.cwd, {
  404  				enableProjectConfig: options?.enableProjectConfig,
  405  				filterExa: options?.filterExa,
  406  				filterBrowser: options?.filterBrowser,
  407  			});
  408  		} catch (error) {
  409  			const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
  410  			options?.onStatus?.({ type: "failed", serverName: ".mcp.json", error: message });
  411  			throw error;

  414  		const result = await this.connectServers(configs, sources, options?.onStatus);

08
L1factconnectors

08 · 可直接吸收 Claude、Gemini、OpenCode 等生态的 MCP 配置

源码事实

capability discovery providers 解析本产品、Claude、Gemini CLI、OpenCode、Codex、Cline、Cursor、VSCode/Windsurf 等配置；OpenCode local/remote 和 Gemini mcpServers 被归一成同一 stdio/SSE/HTTP contract，并保留 source metadata。

白话解释

用户换工具时不必手工重写全部 MCP 配置，OMP 会读取其他 Agent 的配置并统一格式。

对自研 Harness 的含义

迁移体验突出，但跨生态自动加载需有来源可见性和项目 trust。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 88–126
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 127–167
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   92  interface OpenCodeMCPConfig {
   93  	type?: "local" | "remote";
   94  	command?: string | string[];
   95  	args?: string[];
   96  	env?: Record<string, string>;
   97  	environment?: Record<string, string>;

   99  	headers?: Record<string, string>;

  104  function stringArray(value: unknown): string[] | undefined {
  105  	if (!Array.isArray(value)) return undefined;
  106  	for (const item of value) {
  107  		if (typeof item !== "string") return undefined;
  108  	}
  109  	return value;
  110  }
  111
  112  function stringRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, string> | undefined {
  113  	if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) return undefined;
  114
  115  	const record: Record<string, string> = {};
  116  	for (const [key, item] of Object.entries(value)) {
  117  		if (typeof item !== "string") return undefined;
  118  		record[key] = item;
  119  	}
  120  	return record;
  121  }
  122
  123  function normalizeCommand(
  124  	commandValue: string | string[] | undefined,
  125  	argsValue: unknown,
  126  ): { command: string | undefined; args: string[] | undefined } {
  127  	const configuredArgs = stringArray(argsValue);
  128  	if (Array.isArray(commandValue)) {
  129  		const [command, ...commandArgs] = commandValue;
  130  		const args = configuredArgs ? [...commandArgs, ...configuredArgs] : commandArgs;
  131  		return {
  132  			command: typeof command === "string" ? command : undefined,
  133  			args: args.length > 0 ? args : undefined,
  134  		};
  135  	}
  136
  137  	return {
  138  		command: typeof commandValue === "string" ? commandValue : undefined,
  139  		args: configuredArgs && configuredArgs.length > 0 ? configuredArgs : undefined,
  140  	};
  141  }

  143  async function loadMCPServers(ctx: LoadContext): Promise<LoadResult<MCPServer>> {
  144  	const items: MCPServer[] = [];
  145  	const warnings: string[] = [];
  146
  147  	// User-level: ~/.config/opencode/opencode.json
  148  	const userConfigPath = getUserPath(ctx, "opencode", "opencode.json");
  149  	if (userConfigPath) {
  150  		const config = await loadJsonConfig(userConfigPath);
  151  		if (config) {
  152  			const result = extractMCPServers(config, userConfigPath, "user");
  153  			items.push(...result.items);
  154  			if (result.warnings) warnings.push(...result.warnings);
  155  		}
  156  	}
  157
  158  	// Project-level: opencode.json in project root
  159  	const projectConfigPath = path.join(ctx.cwd, "opencode.json");
  160  	const projectConfig = await loadJsonConfig(projectConfigPath);
  161  	if (projectConfig) {
  162  		const result = extractMCPServers(projectConfig, projectConfigPath, "project");
  163  		items.push(...result.items);
  164  		if (result.warnings) warnings.push(...result.warnings);
  165  	}
  166
  167  	return { items, warnings };
  168  }
  169
  170  function extractMCPServers(
  171  	config: Record<string, unknown>,
  172  	configPath: string,
  173  	level: "user" | "project",
  174  ): LoadResult<MCPServer> {
  175  	const items: MCPServer[] = [];
  176  	const warnings: string[] = [];
  177
  178  	if (!config.mcp || typeof config.mcp !== "object") {
  179  		return { items, warnings };
  180  	}
  181
  182  	const servers = expandEnvVarsDeep(config.mcp as Record<string, unknown>);
  183
  184  	for (const [name, raw] of Object.entries(servers)) {
  185  		if (!raw || typeof raw !== "object") {
  186  			warnings.push(`Invalid MCP config for "${name}" in ${configPath}`);
  187  			continue;
  188  		}
  189
  190  		const serverConfig = raw as OpenCodeMCPConfig;
  191
  192  		// Determine transport from OpenCode's "type" field
  193  		let transport: "stdio" | "sse" | "http" | undefined;
  194  		if (serverConfig.type === "local") {
  195  			transport = "stdio";
  196  		} else if (serverConfig.type === "remote") {
  197  			transport = "http";
  198  		} else if (serverConfig.url) {
  199  			transport = "http";
  200  		} else if (serverConfig.command) {
  201  			transport = "stdio";
  202  		}
  203
  204  		const command = normalizeCommand(serverConfig.command, serverConfig.args);
  205  		const env = stringRecord(serverConfig.environment) ?? stringRecord(serverConfig.env);
  206
  207  		items.push({
  208  			name,
  209  			command: command.command,
  210  			args: command.args,
  211  			env,
  212  			url: typeof serverConfig.url === "string" ? serverConfig.url : undefined,
  213  			headers: serverConfig.headers && typeof serverConfig.headers === "object" ? serverConfig.headers : undefined,
  214  			enabled: serverConfig.enabled,
  215  			timeout: typeof serverConfig.timeout === "number" ? serverConfig.timeout : undefined,
  216  			transport,
  217  			_source: createSourceMeta(PROVIDER_ID, configPath, level),
  218  		});
  219  	}
  220
  221  	return { items, warnings };
  222  }
  223

  225  // Skills (skills/)

  238  				providerId: PROVIDER_ID,

   90  function getConfigSources(ctx: LoadContext): OpenCodeConfigSource[] {
   91  	const sources: OpenCodeConfigSource[] = [];
   92  	for (const filename of CONFIG_FILENAMES) {
   93  		const configPath = getUserPath(ctx, "opencode", filename);
   94  		if (configPath) sources.push({ path: configPath, level: "user" });
   95  	}
   96  	for (const filename of CONFIG_FILENAMES) {
   97  		sources.push({ path: path.join(ctx.cwd, filename), level: "project" });

  167  function normalizeCommand(

  169  	argsValue: unknown,

  171  	const configuredArgs = stringArray(argsValue);

  186
  187  async function loadMCPServers(ctx: LoadContext): Promise<LoadResult<MCPServer>> {
  188  	const warnings: string[] = [];
  189
  190  	// Deep-merge each server across config layers in ascending precedence, the
  191  	// way OpenCode itself merges configs, so a partial higher-precedence override
  192  	// (e.g. project opencode.jsonc setting only mcp.<name>.timeout) inherits the
  193  	// command/url from lower-precedence layers instead of shadowing the complete
  194  	// definition and being rejected by mcpCapability.validate.
  195  	const mergedByName = new Map<string, Record<string, unknown>>();
  196  	const sourceByName = new Map<string, OpenCodeConfigSource>();
  197
  198  	for (const source of getConfigSources(ctx)) {
  199  		const config = await loadJsonConfig(source.path, configPath => {
  200  			logger.warn("Failed to parse OpenCode config", { path: configPath });
  201  		});
  202  		if (!config || !isRecord(config.mcp)) continue;
  203
  204  		for (const name in config.mcp) {
  205  			const raw = config.mcp[name];
  206  			if (!isRecord(raw)) {
  207  				warnings.push(`Invalid MCP config for "${name}" in ${source.path}`);
  208  				continue;
  209  			}
  210  			const previous = mergedByName.get(name);
  211  			mergedByName.set(name, previous ? mergeConfigRecords(previous, raw) : raw);
  212  			sourceByName.set(name, source);
  213  		}
  214  	}
  215
  216  	const items: MCPServer[] = [];
  217  	for (const [name, config] of mergedByName) {
  218  		const serverConfig = expandEnvVarsDeep(config) as OpenCodeMCPConfig;
  219  		const source = sourceByName.get(name)!;
  220  		items.push(buildMCPServer(name, serverConfig, source));
  221  	}
  222
  223  	return { items, warnings };
  224  }
  225
  226  /** Deep-merge two OpenCode config records; `override` wins, nested records recurse. */
  227  function mergeConfigRecords(base: Record<string, unknown>, override: Record<string, unknown>): Record<string, unknown> {
  228  	const result: Record<string, unknown> = { ...base };
  229  	for (const key in override) {
  230  		const value = override[key];
  231  		const existing = result[key];
  232  		result[key] = isRecord(existing) && isRecord(value) ? mergeConfigRecords(existing, value) : value;
  233  	}
  234  	return result;
  235  }
  236
  237  /** Translate one merged OpenCode MCP entry into the canonical MCPServer shape. */
  238  function buildMCPServer(name: string, serverConfig: OpenCodeMCPConfig, source: OpenCodeConfigSource): MCPServer {

  183  			? ((args as Partial<AstEditSchemaInfer>).paths as string[])
  184  			: [];
  185  		return paths.length > 0 && paths.every(path => isInternalUrlPath(path)) ? "read" : "write";
  186  	};
  187  	readonly formatApprovalDetails = (args: unknown): string[] => {
  188  		const params = args as Partial<AstEditSchemaInfer>;
  189  		const lines: string[] = [];
  190  		const ops = Array.isArray(params.ops) ? params.ops : [];
  191  		const firstOp = ops[0];
  192  		if (firstOp) {
  193  			lines.push(`Pattern: ${truncateForPrompt(firstOp.pat)}`);
  194  			lines.push(`Replacement: ${truncateForPrompt(firstOp.out)}`);
  195  			if (ops.length > 1) {

  197  			}
  198  		}
  199  		if (Array.isArray(params.paths) && params.paths.length > 0) {
  200  			lines.push(`Paths: ${truncateForPrompt(params.paths.join(", "))}`);
  201  		}
  202  		return lines;
  203  	};

bash

模型可调用的内置工具 · tool registry · packages/coding-agent/src/tools/bash.ts:545
源码 ↗

Executes bash commands with optional timeout and working directory.

bash→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
  540   * Bash tool implementation.
  541   *
  542   * Executes bash commands with optional timeout and working directory.
  543   */
  544  export class BashTool implements AgentTool<typeof bashSchemaBase | typeof bashSchemaWithAsync, BashToolDetails> {
  545  	readonly name = "bash";
  546  	readonly approval = (args: unknown): ToolApprovalDecision => {
  547  		const rawCommand = (args as Partial<BashToolInput>).command;
  548  		const command = typeof rawCommand === "string" ? rawCommand : "";
  549  		const patternRules = getBashApprovalPatternRules(this.session.settings.get("bash.patterns"));
  550  		const patternRule = findBashApprovalPatternRule(command, patternRules);
  551  		if (patternRule?.approval === "deny") {
  552  			return {
  553  				tier: "exec",
  554  				override: true,
  555  				policy: "deny",
  556  				reason: `Blocked by bash pattern: ${patternRule.match}`,
  557  			};
  558  		}
  559  		if (command !== "" && CRITICAL_BASH_PATTERNS.some(pattern => pattern.test(command))) {
  560  			return { tier: "exec", override: true, reason: "Critical pattern detected" };
  561  		}
  562  		if (patternRule?.approval === "allow") return { tier: "write", policy: "allow" };
  563  		if (patternRule?.approval === "prompt") {
  564  			return {
  565  				tier: "exec",
  566  				override: true,
  567  				policy: "prompt",
  568  				reason: `Prompt required by bash pattern: ${patternRule.match}`,
  569  			};
  570  		}
  571  		return "exec";
  572  	};
  573  	readonly formatApprovalDetails = (args: unknown): string[] => {
  574  		const rawCommand = (args as Partial<BashToolInput>).command;
  575  		const command = typeof rawCommand === "string" ? rawCommand : "(missing)";
  576  		return [`Command: ${truncateForPrompt(command)}`];
  577  	};
  578  	readonly label = "Bash";
  579  	readonly loadMode = "essential";
  580  	get description(): string {
  581  		const evalBackends = resolveEvalBackends(this.session);
  582  		const isToolActive = (name: string, fallback: boolean): boolean => this.session.isToolActive?.(name) ?? fallback;
  583  		return prompt.render(bashDescription, {
  584  			asyncEnabled: this.#asyncEnabled,
  585  			autoBackgroundEnabled: this.#autoBackgroundEnabled,
  586  			autoBackgroundThresholdSeconds: Math.max(0, Math.floor(this.#autoBackgroundThresholdMs / 1000)),
  587  			hasAstGrep: isToolActive("ast_grep", this.session.settings.get("astGrep.enabled")),
  588  			hasAstEdit: isToolActive("ast_edit", this.session.settings.get("astEdit.enabled")),
  589  			hasGrep: isToolActive("grep", this.session.settings.get("grep.enabled")),
  590  			hasGlob: isToolActive("glob", this.session.settings.get("glob.enabled")),
  591  			hasRead: isToolActive("read", true),
  592  			hasLaunch: isToolActive("hub", this.session.settings.get("launch.enabled")),
  593  			hasEval: isToolActive(
  594  				"eval",
  595  				evalBackends.python || evalBackends.js || evalBackends.ruby || evalBackends.julia,
  596  			),
  597  			hasShellBuiltins: !shellBuiltinsDisabled(this.session.settings),
  598  			isWindows: process.platform === "win32",
  599  		});
  600  	}
  601  	readonly parameters: BashToolSchema;
  602  	// Non-pty calls run alongside each other (the executor isolates overlapping
  603  	// runs on the same shell session); pty takes over the terminal UI and must
  604  	// run alone.

   34  /**
   35   * Direct create/update/delete of isolated managed skills. Gated behind
   36   * `autolearn.enabled`; backend-independent (the skill side is standalone).
   37   */
   38  export class ManageSkillTool implements AgentTool<typeof manageSkillSchema> {
   39  	readonly name = "manage_skill";
   40  	readonly approval = "write" as const;
   41  	readonly label = "Manage Skill";
   42  	readonly description = manageSkillDescription;
   43  	readonly parameters = manageSkillSchema;
   44  	readonly strict = true;
   45  	readonly loadMode = "essential" as const;
   46  	readonly summary = "Create, update, or delete an isolated managed skill";
   47
   48  	constructor(private readonly refreshSkills?: () => Promise<void>) {}
   49
   50  	static createIf(session: ToolSession): ManageSkillTool | null {
   51  		if (!session.settings.get("autolearn.enabled")) return null;
   52  		return new ManageSkillTool(session.refreshSkills);
   53  	}
   54
   55  	async execute(_id: string, params: ManageSkillParams): Promise<AgentToolResult> {
   56  		if (params.action === "delete") {
   57  			await deleteManagedSkill(params.name);
   58  			await this.refreshSkills?.();
   59  			return {
   60  				content: [{ type: "text", text: `Deleted managed skill "${params.name}".` }],
   61  				details: { action: "delete", name: params.name },
   62  			};
   63  		}
   64
   65  		// Defensive narrowing: the schema refine already rejects create/update
   66  		// without both fields, so this is unreachable for valid input — it only
   67  		// proves the strings are present to `writeManagedSkill`'s typed contract.
   68  		if (!params.description || !params.body) {
   69  			throw new Error(`"${params.action}" requires both "description" and "body".`);
   70  		}
   71  		// A managed skill resolves below any authored skill of the same name
   72  		// (authored always wins in discovery), so creating one under a name an
   73  		// authored skill already claims writes a file that never surfaces. Refuse
   74  		// up front rather than report a false "Created". `sanitizeSkillName`
   75  		// normalizes to the on-disk name the discovery scan compares against.
   76  		if (params.action === "create" && isNameClaimedByAuthoredSkill(sanitizeSkillName(params.name))) {
   77  			return {
   78  				content: [
   79  					{
   80  						type: "text",
   81  						text: `Cannot create managed skill "${params.name}": an authored skill of that name already exists, and managed skills cannot override authored ones. Choose a different name.`,
   82  					},
   83  				],
   84  				isError: true,
   85  				details: { action: "create", name: params.name, shadowed: true },
   86  			};
   87  		}
   88  		const { path: skillPath } = await writeManagedSkill({
   89  			action: params.action,
   90  			name: params.name,
   91  			description: params.description,
   92  			body: params.body,
   93  		});
   94  		await this.refreshSkills?.();
   95  		const relativePath = path.relative(getManagedSkillsDir(), skillPath);
   96  		const verb = params.action === "create" ? "Created" : "Updated";
   97  		return {
   98  			content: [{ type: "text", text: `${verb} managed skill "${params.name}" (managed-skills/${relativePath}).` }],

   16  export class MemoryEditTool implements AgentTool<typeof memoryEditSchema> {
   17  	readonly name = "memory_edit";
   18  	readonly approval = "read" as const;
   19  	readonly label = "Memory Edit";
   20  	readonly description = memoryEditDescription;
   21  	readonly parameters = memoryEditSchema;
   22  	readonly strict = true;
   23  	readonly loadMode = "discoverable";
   24  	readonly summary = "Update, forget, or invalidate Mnemopi memories";
   25

   28  	static createIf(session: ToolSession): MemoryEditTool | null {
   29  		const backend = session.settings.get("memory.backend");
   30  		if (backend !== "mnemopi") return null;
   31  		return new MemoryEditTool(session);
   32  	}
   33
   34  	async execute(_id: string, params: MemoryEditParams): Promise<AgentToolResult> {
   35  		const state = this.session.getMnemopiSessionState?.();
   36  		if (!state) {
   37  			throw new Error("Mnemopi backend is not initialised for this session.");
   38  		}
   39  		if (params.op === "update" && params.content === undefined && params.importance === undefined) {
   40  			throw new Error("memory_edit update requires content or importance.");
   41  		}
   42
   43  		const importance = params.importance === undefined ? undefined : Math.max(0, Math.min(1, params.importance));
   44  		const result = state.editScopedMemory(params.op, params.id, {
   45  			content: params.content,
   46  			importance,
   47  			replacementId: params.replacement_id,
   48  		});
   49  		const location = result.bank ? ` in bank ${result.bank}${result.store ? ` (${result.store})` : ""}` : "";
   50  		const text =
   51  			result.status === "not_found"
   52  				? `Memory ${params.id} was not found${location}.`
   53  				: result.status === "not_editable"
   54  					? `Memory ${params.id} is a read-only fact${location}; it cannot be edited. Read it with memory://${params.id}.`
   55  					: `Memory ${params.id} ${result.status}${location}.`;
   56  		return {
   57  			content: [{ type: "text", text }],
   58  			details: result,
   59  		};
   60  	}
   61  }

  429  	 */

  441  	/**
  442  	 * Whether the agent can actually reach `inspect_image` right now: exposed
  443  	 * top-level, or mounted as an `xd://` device while the effective mode wants
  444  	 * it (mounted devices stay executable via `write xd://inspect_image`, so a
  445  	 * metadata-only read remains actionable). Sessions with neither
  446  	 * availability signal (tests, embedded use) fall back to the mode
  447  	 * computation alone. Restricted slates (subagents without the tool and
  448  	 * without xdev) resolve to unavailable, so those sessions get inline image
  449  	 * blocks instead of guidance pointing at an absent tool.
  450  	 */
  451  	#resolveInspectImageAvailability(): boolean {
  452  		const topLevel = this.session.isToolActive?.("inspect_image");
  453  		const xdev = this.session.xdev;
  454  		if (topLevel === undefined && xdev === undefined) return isInspectImageToolActive(this.session);
  455  		if (topLevel === true) return true;
  456  		return xdev?.mountedNames.has("inspect_image") === true && isInspectImageToolActive(this.session);
  457  	}

  163  					planId: requireValue(params.plan_id, "plan_id"),
  164  				});

  233  export const ttsTool: CustomTool<typeof ttsSchema, TtsToolDetails> = {
  234  	name: "tts",
  235  	label: "Speech Generation",
  236  	strict: false,
  237  	approval: "write",
  238  	description:
  239  		"Generate a speech audio file from text and write it to output_path. Two backends, selected by the providers.tts setting (auto|local|xai): " +
  240  		`local = on-device neural TTS (Kokoro-82M via the bundled ONNX runtime, no network, output is always WAV/PCM16; voice set by the tts.localVoice setting — ${KOKORO_VOICES.map(v => (v.id === DEFAULT_TTS_VOICE ? `${v.id} (default)` : v.id)).join(", ")}); ` +
  241  		`xai = xAI Grok Voice cloud (built-in voices: ${formatVoiceList()}; custom voice IDs accepted; MP3 or WAV). ` +
  242  		"auto prefers local, but routes an .mp3 request to xAI when credentials exist (only the cloud path emits MP3); " +
  243  		"otherwise an .mp3 path is written as a sibling .wav. xAI codec is inferred from the output_path suffix. " +
  244  		`Max ${XAI_MAX_TEXT_LENGTH.toLocaleString("en-US")} characters.`,
  245  	parameters: ttsSchema,
  246  	async execute(
  247  		_toolCallId: string,
  248  		params: TtsSchemaType,
  249  		_onUpdate,
  250  		ctx: CustomToolContext,
  251  		signal?: AbortSignal,
  252  	): Promise<AgentToolResult<TtsToolDetails, TtsSchemaType>> {
  253  		const cwd = ctx.sessionManager.getCwd();
  254  		const outputPath = resolveToCwd(params.output_path, cwd);
  255  		const displayPath = formatPathRelativeToCwd(outputPath, cwd);
  256  		const codec: TtsCodec = outputPath.toLowerCase().endsWith(".wav") ? "wav" : "mp3";
  257
  258  		const preference = readStringSetting("providers.tts") ?? "auto";
  259  		// Only resolve xAI creds when they can affect routing (skip for an explicit local preference).
  260  		const hasXaiCreds =
  261  			preference === "local" ? false : (await resolveXAIHttpCredentials(ctx.modelRegistry)) !== null;
  262  		const backend = resolveTtsBackend({ preference, wantsMp3: codec === "mp3", hasXaiCreds });
  263
  264  		if (backend === "local") return synthesizeLocal(params, cwd, outputPath, signal);
  265  		return synthesizeXai(params, ctx, outputPath, displayPath, codec, signal);
  266  	},
  267  };

write

模型可调用的内置工具 · tool registry · packages/coding-agent/src/tools/write.ts:502
源码 ↗

Creates or overwrites files with optional LSP formatting and diagnostics.

write→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
  497   * Write tool implementation.
  498   *
  499   * Creates or overwrites files with optional LSP formatting and diagnostics.
  500   */
  501  export class WriteTool implements AgentTool<typeof writeSchema, WriteToolDetails> {
  502  	readonly name = "write";
  503  	readonly approval = (args: unknown): ToolApprovalDecision => {
  504  		const rawPath = (args as Partial<WriteParams>).path;
  505  		if (typeof rawPath !== "string") return "write";
  506  		// Unwrap a hashline `[path#TAG]` wrapper first (parity with execute) so a
  507  		// wrapped `[ssh://h/x#ABCD]` can't dodge scheme detection and the tier checks below.
  508  		const path = unwrapHashlineHeaderPath(rawPath);
  509  		// xd:// device writes execute the mounted tool — take its approval tier.
  510  		// The resolution devices (xd://resolve, xd://reject, xd://propose)
  511  		// finalize a staged, already-previewed action, so they stay at read tier.
  512  		const xdevTarget = parseXdUrl(path);
  513  		if (xdevTarget) {
  514  			if (xdevTarget.name === REPORT_ISSUE_DEVICE_NAME) return "write";
  515  			if (xdevTarget.name && isResolutionDeviceName(xdevTarget.name)) return "read";
  516  			const inst =
  517  				xdevTarget.name && this.session.xdev ? resolveXdevTool(this.session.xdev, xdevTarget.name) : undefined;
  518  			if (!inst) return "exec";
  519  			// Decode the device JSON payload and evaluate the mounted tool's own
  520  			// approval (which may be argument-dependent, e.g. ast_edit is read-tier
  521  			// for internal-URL paths, debug is read-tier for inspection actions).
  522  			// Malformed JSON, non-object payloads, missing content, and approval
  523  			// functions that reject schema-invalid objects stay exec so the gate
  524  			// fails closed — the dispatch itself rejects invalid arguments too.
  525  			const rawContent = (args as Partial<WriteParams>).content;
  526  			if (typeof rawContent !== "string") return "exec";
  527  			let parsed: unknown;
  528  			try {
  529  				parsed = JSON.parse(rawContent);
  530  			} catch {
  531  				return "exec";
  532  			}
  533  			if (!isRecord(parsed)) return "exec";
  534  			try {
  535  				// The tier is the mounted tool's own (argument-dependent) approval; the
  536  				// policyKey makes the outer gate consult `tools.approval.<device>` for
  537  				// this dispatch before falling back to `tools.approval.write`, so users
  538  				// can scope allow/deny/prompt to a single device (issue #7923).
  539  				return { tier: resolveToolTier(inst, parsed), policyKey: xdevTarget.name! };
  540  			} catch {
  541  				return "exec";
  542  			}
  543  		}
  544  		// Remote SSH writes open an outbound connection and run a remote shell —
  545  		// gate them like the exec-tier `ssh` tool, ahead of the handler-write
  546  		// logic. Substring match also covers selector-suffixed targets.
  547  		if (pathTargetsSsh(path)) return "exec";
  548  		if (!isInternalUrlPath(path)) return "write";
  549  		// Internal URLs are usually session-local artifacts (read tier), but a
  550  		// scheme whose handler exposes a `write` hook mutates handler-owned user
  551  		// data (e.g. vault:// notes) and must take the write tier so always-ask
  552  		// mode actually prompts.
  553  		const match = /^([a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*):\/\//i.exec(path.trim());
  554  		const handler = match ? InternalUrlRouter.instance().getHandler(match[1]!.toLowerCase()) : undefined;
  555  		return handler?.write ? "write" : "read";
  556  	};
  557  	readonly formatApprovalDetails = (args: unknown): string[] => {
  558  		const params = args as Partial<WriteParams>;
  559  		const targetPath = typeof params.path === "string" ? params.path : "(missing)";
  560  		const content = typeof params.content === "string" ? params.content : "";
  561  		return [`Path: ${truncateForPrompt(targetPath)}`, `Content:\n${truncateForPrompt(content)}`];
打开这条指令的动态执行图 →

  219  		'Use `result: { data: <your output> }` for success, or `result: { error: "message" }` for failure. Keep the `result` wrapper.';

  248  			if (validator) {

  250  				validateSection = validator.validateSection;
  251  				rejectUnknownSections = validator.rejectUnknownSections;
  252  				knownSectionLabels = validator.knownSectionLabels;
  253  				isKnownSection = label => validator.isKnownSection(label);
  254  			}
  255
  256  			const schemaHint = formatSchema(normalizedSchema ?? session.outputSchema);
  257  			const schemaDescription = schemaError
  258  				? `Structured JSON output (output schema invalid; accepting unconstrained object): ${schemaError}`
  259  				: `Structured output matching the schema:\n${schemaHint}`;
  260  			let sanitizedSchema: Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
  261  			if (!schemaError && normalizedSchema !== undefined) {
  262  				const strictProbe = tryEnforceStrictSchema(normalizedSchema);
  263  				if (strictProbe.strict) {
  264  					sanitizedSchema = sanitizeSchemaForStrictMode(normalizedSchema);
  265  				} else {
  266  					sanitizedSchema = normalizedSchema;
  267  					this.strict = false;
  268  				}
  269  			} else if (!schemaError && normalized === true) {
  270  				sanitizedSchema = {};
  271  				this.strict = false;
  272  			}

grievances
